## Exit Reporting — Implementation Steps in Code (`2026-11-11`, developer reference)

### Step 1: Add `exit_report_log()` method to `PositionManager`

File: `position_manager.py`, inside the `PositionManager` class

```python
def exit_report_log(self, ticket, action, old_sl, new_sl, old_tp, new_tp,
                    r_now, door, reason, bid, ask) -> None:
```

### Step 2: Call `exit_report_log()` at Door 1 — Disaster Stop

Where initial SL/TP is set or tightened in `_manage_one()`:
```python
self.exit_report_log(
    ticket=ticket, action="DISASTER", old_sl=old_sl, new_sl=new_sl,
    old_tp=old_tp, new_tp=new_tp, r_now=r_now, door="Door 1",
    reason="initial structural SL/TP at entry", bid=bid, ask=ask
)
```

### Step 3: Call `exit_report_log()` at Door 2 — Breakeven

When `r_now >= PM_BE_TRIGGER_R` (0.75) and `not be_done`:
```python
self.exit_report_log(
    ticket=ticket, action="BE", old_sl=cur_sl, new_sl=entry,
    old_tp=cur_tp, new_tp=cur_tp, r_now=r_now, door="Door 2",
    reason=f"breakeven triggered at +{r_now:.2f}R", bid=bid, ask=ask
)
```

### Step 4: Call `exit_report_log()` at Door 3 — Partial Profit

When `r_now >= PM_PARTIAL_TRIGGER_R` (2.0) and partial not yet taken:
```python
self.exit_report_log(
    ticket=ticket, action="PARTIAL", old_sl=cur_sl, new_sl=new_sl,
    old_tp=cur_tp, new_tp=new_tp, r_now=r_now, door="Door 3",
    reason=f"banked half at +{r_now:.2f}R", bid=bid, ask=ask
)
```

### Step 5: Call `exit_report_log()` at Door 4 — Trailing Runner

When price moves favorably after Door 3:
```python
self.exit_report_log(
    ticket=ticket, action="TRAIL", old_sl=cur_sl, new_sl=new_sl,
    old_tp=cur_tp, new_tp=cur_tp, r_now=r_now, door="Door 4",
    reason=f"trailing runner at 1.0×ATR, +{r_now:.2f}R", bid=bid, ask=ask
)
```

### Step 6: Call `exit_report_log()` at Door 5 — Time Stop

When age >= limit and `r_now < 1.0`:
```python
self.exit_report_log(
    ticket=ticket, action="TIME_STOP", old_sl=cur_sl, new_sl=cur_sl,
    old_tp=cur_tp, new_tp=cur_tp, r_now=r_now, door="Door 5",
    reason=f"no +1.0R after {eff_time_stop:.0f} min, close at market",
    bid=bid, ask=ask
)
```

### Step 7: Test and ship

1. Add the method and all 5 call sites
2. Run `$PY -m pytest -q` → expect `738 passed, 3 subtests`
3. Run pinned replay `--dates 2026-11-03..2026-11-09` → verify exit reports appear
4. Ship as isolated stage patch (`--check` + `apply` + `cmp`)
5. **One loop restart** after install
6. Review after 30 days of demo — keep or remove per keep-it-out test

## Step 2d — POWER x MTF ENTRY (`2026-11-11`, owner-directed: "keep this topic in the Book and the to-do")

**Why (tape, `replay-37`):** `[9]` GO n=36 −0.06 pts/sig vs SKIPPED-WAIT n=57 **+1.86** — the waiting hand already knows more than the shooting hand. `[8b]` 51/57 waits = `POWER v2 says NEITHER`: the gate has no opinion because it reads only ONE chart (M3, `AI_PROMPT_BARS=4`) — it lacks information, not courage. GO is worst at +3m (−0.48) and +6m (−0.67), recovers after +9m: M3 confirms late → we enter at M3-exhaustion. Owner design call: give POWER 2–3 timeframes.

**Phase 0 — measurement (judge-side ONLY; `tools/raw_replay.py` new section `[8j]`, read-only, ship-side untouched; same pinned dates):**
- Per historical signal: sign(M15 trend/MA stack), M15 nearest structural wall vs own TP, sign(M3) agreement, M1 range/ATR ratio to M3, plus each team's per-TF score.
- Buckets: `M15 x M3` agree/oppose grid, `wall-ahead < 1.2xATR` vs clear, M1 range 0.5/1.0/2.0 x M3 — forward pts at +3/+6/+9/+12/+21/+45 min per bucket, n shown.
- Deliverable = the table that decides the design. No gate built from anything not in this table. `[8j]` text only, no browser-report change.

**Phase 1 — gate spec (written AFTER grid exists, thresholds fixed BEFORE first score):**
- M3 stays the SHOOTER. M15 = REFEREE: direction vote + wall-ahead veto. M1 = RULER, not judge: sizes fence/doors/tick-entry; a directional M1 vote allowed only if the grid proves it.
- `POWER v2` keeps Law #11: per-TF scores computed separately, no negotiation, AI never re-weights; veto power lives in the gate table, never in the AI prompt.
- Grid outcomes -> pre-registered rules, e.g. both signs agree -> GO; referee opposes -> WAIT; `NEITHER` + referee clear -> measured (maybe GO with small size). `NEITHER` must get a real meaning instead of a shrug.
- Mirror the gate as an `[8m]` scoreboard row (episode-deduped); bar = new `GO` cohort must beat old-gate GO on the SAME signals **and on both day windows (3d and 5d)**. Phase-1 first move = `[8m]` WRONGFUL-VETO RE-SCORE (episode-deduped) of the blocked cohorts, offline only.

**Phase 2 — sizing (M1 scalper geometry):** fence from M1 noise (1.0xATR_M1 ~ 2.5 pts on this week's sea) + wall shelter; doors sized in ATR units of the SAME TF as the fence — Step 2 autopsy law: **no door trigger beyond own TP; no teleport inside 1xATR of entry**. Ship order: gate scores win offline first, sizing second — never bundled.

**Acceptance bar (pre-registered, never bent):** the new entry must beat old POWER v2 on the same signals over the same pinned tape at n>=30; else the old entry stays. First ship touch only after Phase 0 numbers exist AND a green shadow score exists.

**Phase 0 RESULT (`replay-38`, `2026-11-11`, 3 pinned days 11-05/06/09, tool `feda784`/`5d210d23…`, rig=GitHub=workspace by hash):** MTF grids N=69/69 actionable (GO 30, WAIT 39; 36 waits still `POWER v2 says NEITHER`). (a) **DIRECTION-REFEREE HYPOTHESIS = NULL on this tape:** in BOTH M12xM3 primary and M15xM3 comparison grids the agree cells are the worst (`M12 UP x M3 UP FIX -0.53`; `M12 DOWN x M3 FLAT -1.03`; oppose cells better: fwd +2.77/+2.93, n=6/9 thin); M1 x M3 audition: aligned M1 row = worst big cell (FIX -3.44, n=21), MIXED row best (+1.73 fwd, n=24). Verdict = *no evidence to build the multi-TF directional vote* — not proof it can never work; frozen, evidence-bound. (b) **REAL SUSPECT = GATE VETO BLOCKS:** `[2]` per-cycle means `Climax block +4.80/sig (n=9)`, `Non-Trend BUY guard +4.80 (n=6)`, `POWER Opposes +1.33 (n=9)` vs GO +1.30; `Dead-Zone -4.87` + `NEITHER -1.03` stay blocked. Cycle-counted = double-count → honest re-score needed (`[8m]` episode-dedup, next patch). (c) Wall hint: tight +6.26 FIX (n=4) = noise. (d) Sea sizes: Avg ATR M1=3.24 / M3=5.47 / M12=9.87 → Phase-2 scalper geometry grounded (M1 fence ~3 pts vs current avg 10.4). (e) 3-day champion bracket = +39.1 vs 5-day -2.1 = pure day-selection power → both-window rule (Hygiene #2).
**Phase 1 v2 RESULT (`replay-40`, `2026-11-11`, same 3 days, tool `4d5d3f01…`): Hygiene #6 PASSED — `[8l]`+`[8c]` byte-identical to `replay-39` (verified by `diff`). Episode-accurate counts (GO control: 30 cyc -> 8 EP +15.1 = +1.89/ep). LEAK SUSPECTS SURVIVE HONEST COUNT (all [THIN]): `Non-Trend BUY guard` +21.3 (2 EP, +10.63/ep), `Late POWER Climax` +14.4 (3 EP, +4.80/ep), `POWER Opposes` +4.1 (3 EP, +1.36/ep) = +39.8 vs GO +15.1. CORRECTLY-BLOCKED (negative EPs, keep): `Family Gate` -60.4 (165 EP), `Dead-Zone` -16.8, `Conf<35` -17.9, `3b-Conflict` -6.9, all three WAIT cohorts negative. Reading: 5 of 8 goalkeeper rules proven right; 3 rules stand wrongful-veto candidates. Next = `[8n]` gate-mirror scored on 3d AND 5d with the same day set for both arms; only 3-day-positive candidates may be tried; bar = beat GO control on BOTH windows, n>=8 episodes per window per touched block else NOT proven.

**RENAME receipt:** grids block shipped labeled `[8j]` — collides with sealed Risk-Manager section; relabel to `[8l]` in next replay patch (bookkeeping, zero logic); gate-mirror row pre-registered as `[8m]` (old text said `[8k]` — also taken by sealed `#12`).

**STATUS SNAPSHOT (`2026-11-11`, night):** replay-38 (Phase 0 grids: direction-referee NULL) + replay-39/40 (veto episode count: 5 goalkeeper rules proven RIGHT incl. Family Gate 165 EP; 3 proven-leak suspects GUILTY-HINT, all [THIN]: Non-Trend BUY +10.63/2 EP, Climax +4.80/3 EP, Opposes +1.36/3 EP). **PENDING:** 5-day tape `replay-41` completes the both-window check of `[8m]` (owner rig). THEN build `[8n]` gate mirror (lift/keep, both arms same day set, bar = beat GO control on 3d AND 5d, >=8 EP/window/touched-block). Book ceiling raised to < 4500.

**PHASE-1 BOTH-WINDOWS VERDICT (`2026-11-12`, `replay-41` 5d vs `replay-40` 3d — CLOSED: GATES STAY):** the wide window flips the suspect table. **Non-Trend BUY (3b Guard) ACQUITTED** — 3d +10.63/ep (2 EP) collapses to +0.13/ep (9 EP): window luck, Hygiene #4 in action. **POWER Opposes (P3) ACQUITTED** — sign flips +1.36 to -0.70/ep. **Late Climax (3d) = the single surviving leak suspect**: positive on BOTH windows (+4.80/ep 3d n=3; +3.63/ep 5d n=5), beats GO control per-episode on both (+1.89 / +0.91) — but below the 8-EP bar, so `[8n]` stays ARMED, not fired; trigger = >=8 Climax EP on both windows (loop adds ~1/day; a `--days 10` tape checks sooner). **Proven-right guards, both windows:** Family Gate (-0.37/ep 3d, -1.40/ep 5d over 268 EP 5/5 days; avoided -60.4 -> -375.1), Session Dead-Zone (-3.36 / -4.29), Conf<35% (-4.47 both, same 4 EP), Conf<50 3b + SNIPER AUTH (negative both). GO control 5d: +8.2 pts / 9 EP / 4-of-5 days; `[8c]` ruler N=36 fixed-wave -2.1 = anchor tie (tape verified line-by-line: `[8m]` cyc totals tie `[2]` numerators; GO 36 = `[8c]` N=36). **Owner order (`2026-11-11`) stands: Step 2e HANDS fix — near-reach TP never moved further away + SL laws + `exit_report_log()` as ONE exit stage — runs next, replay-first, both windows, live untouched until it wins and owner says yes.**

**STEP 2e EXHIBIT A — the Hands' own diary, 2026-11-11 demo (first live capture; `data/management_log.csv`):** both GO trades were SELLS (audit BUY rows = the closing deals), entry ~4017.33 and ~4003.52, 0.01 lot. They peaked at **+$15.84 / +$15.43** (max 31.27) and closed at **+$9.61 / +$11.55** (day +$21.16) — the Hands taxed **32% of the fish**. The owner's real-case memory IS trade #1, to the minute: at 15:32:11 price 4001.49 had ALREADY swum through the original TP 4003.70 (~$13.6 win, missed at 0.9 pts on a 1-min sample grid) — worse, at 15:23:12 `TP_UPDATE` had pulled TP 4003.70->4000.59 while price was 7 pts above, and at 15:32 CHANDELIER pulled it to 3986.91 (30.4 pts = the owner's "$30", reason *TP front-runs fresh opposing structure*) then moved it back to 4000.67 at 15:33 — bucket ping-pong on a <1-minute blip; 15:34:39 a 6.2-pt breath hit the raised SL 4007.68 (+9.61). Diary census for the day: `TP_UPDATE` moved TP away 7x (up to 13.7 pts); `BE` fired early at +0.77R/+0.98R (*"early: the move lacks CVD support"*); `WALL_SHELTER`+`CHANDELIER_WAVE_TRAIL` tightened the same SL from two directions to a 3.8-6.2 pt standoff = smaller than one normal wave-breath. **Five-year-old recap:** the robot reads one small pond and guesses where the fish jump. It guesses wrong often, and when the water looks flat it freezes (“NEITHER”). Next we look at TWO more ponds — the big pond (are the fish swimming our way? any rocks ahead?) and the tiny pond (how big is one wave?) — then we write the rules DOWN, score them in the practice room against the old guesser, and only if it wins does it get on the boat.

## Measurement Hygiene LAWS (`2026-11-11`, born from Step 2 + Step 2d the same day — standing)

1. **Measure before you code.** One pinned replay day kills a whole design before it reaches the boat — two losers prevented this way (R-multiple doors, direction-referee).
2. **Both-window rule.** Any cohort score (gate or exit proposal) is reported on the pinned 3-day AND 5-day windows and must win on BOTH; absolute numbers are window-scoped, never compared across day-sets (same tool: +39.1 on 3d vs -2.1 on 5d).
3. **Per-cycle bucket numbers are HINTS, not verdicts** — a flag held across minutes double-counts; money claims require episode-level dedup (`[8g]` style) before any gate change is proposed.
4. **Thin-cell law: n<8 = direction hint, never a conclusion** — printed inside every grid so a reader cannot miss it.
5. **Size in the sea's units, never in our fear's units** — doors/fences in ATR of the SAME TF as the plan; no door trigger beyond own TP; no BE teleport inside 1xATR of entry (Step 2 autopsy).
6. **Identical old rows = proof of tool honesty.** A measure-only change must leave every pre-existing section bit-identical; if not, the tool (not the market) is guilty first.
7. **Revert failing experiments immediately; commit only judge-side files; state LIVE vs NOT-LIVE explicitly.** An uncommitted failed patch on disk is a loaded gun aimed at the next accidental restart.
8. **Delivery addendum:** the viewer DISPLAYS, it does not transfer — patches must physically land in `Downloads` and be `wc -l` + `sha256sum` verified BEFORE `git apply`; ship ONE patch computed from the rig's CURRENT exact blob, never dependent chains that can half-fail.
9. **Anchors are read, never recalled.** This very update: a script that edited by remembered strings silently no-oped two register lines (caught by post-write grep re-read — the Book's own #1 law applies to the Book itself).
10. **Paths are read from git, never assumed.** (`2026-11-14`, the owner's catch, live:) my BELT-1 block printed `bash: data/HOLY_BOOK.md: No such file or directory` TWICE - I wrote `data/HOLY_BOOK.md` because the repo keeps most artifacts under `data/`, while `git ls-tree -r 99b3cc8` has always said the Book is at the REPO ROOT (`HOLY_BOOK.md`, nothing under `data/`). The folder shape is the owner's knowledge, not my inference: any block touching a repo file must first `git ls-tree`/`ls` it in the same session and paste the path that EXISTS. Two lines of my receipt were empty because of this - and an empty field must be read as "not measured", never as "fine" (item 31).
11. **The Book is a working surface, not an archive.** The owner's instruction of `2026-11-14`: *"keep checking our holy book and keep note and update it as well"*. So: at the START of a code day read the open sections (STEP 2g, the STRATEGY ledger, 3.5a-e) - they are live findings, not history; at the END of a code day write the receipt (numbers from paper, laws with their catch, and my own errors in the same section); and whenever a rule changes HOW work is done (LAWS #13, #14, the ±80 mapper law, "the hall stays off until the owner signs"), it goes in as a LAW, not as a chat message. A discovery that lives only in a reply does not exist.
12. **A machine-readable record must contain no run-volatile prose.** (`2026-11-14`, BELT-1: the owner's rerun test printed `determinism=DIFFERENT` and, on my box, `DIFFERING FIELDS: ['mirror']` - ONE field of forty, `episodes` and `_days` byte-equal.) Timestamps, quarantine/temp folder names, `.N` retry suffixes and "N earlier rehearsals" counters make a paper unrepeatable even when the MEASUREMENT is perfectly repeatable - and then the one test that could catch a real leak cries wolf and gets ignored. Put volatile prose in the LOG; keep the json a pure function of the input. Enforced by the cheapest test we own: sail the same day twice, `cmp` the files, and let PASS mean BYTES.

### STEP 2f PRE-REGISTRATION — THE ELASTIC ROPE (`2026-11-12`, written BEFORE the tape; owner's own design)
**The idea:** keep the SL line trailing the peak, but the gap is not constant - it shrinks the further price has run from entry (more profit earned = less slack allowed). Smooth (no rungs), ratchet-only (never loosens), TP frozen at the wave (Law #1).
**Arm in the ruler (tool blob `a8925e51`, row `Step 2f Preview: ELASTIC TRAIL on FIXED WAVE`):** gap = max(0.5 x opening-R, (1.5 - 0.25 x peak_R) x ATR_run); moves decided on closed-M3 peaks; exits against lines valid at bar start (SL before TP, no intra-bar peek); universe = the champion's own fix_out-eligible cycles with sl_fix/tp_fix + run-constant ATR - exactly the ladder arm's geometry, so row-vs-Benchmark is one-variable fair.
**The bar, fixed now, no re-tuning EVER:** on BOTH pinned seas (`--dates 2026-11-03,2026-11-04,2026-11-05,2026-11-06,2026-11-09` and `--dates 2026-11-09,2026-11-10,2026-11-11`) the elastic row must beat `Benchmark: 2.0x SL / 3.5x TP Fixed Wave` (-2.1 A / +82.0 B). The ladder row stays printed as the archive reference. **Win both** -> live arm proposal may be drafted (behind flag, with `exit_report_log()` finally built to justify it). **Lose either** -> rope-tightening retires with the ladder: this engine's exit = anchor well + hands off, no further rope chapters on borrowed seas.
**Prediction on record (made before, so it can be caught):** loses sea A (chop eats slack); on sea B it may claw near the champion but not past it - "close" is not "beats", and only beats ships.

## STRATEGY (open ledger - ordered, append-only; started `2026-11-12`, owner instruction: keep every strategy conversation HERE, in THIS order; re-visit after the big system change, together with the STEP 2g checkpoint)
**Order of study, fixed by owner (do not reorder):** 1) Auction, Imbalance, Balance. 2) Volume Profile, VAH, VAL, POC, session VWAP + anchored VWAP, resistance/support lines. 3) Delta, Absorption, Exhaustion in order flow. Sources consulted for these definitions: the Market Profile/AMT tradition (Steidlmayer, CBOT 1980s) via current practitioner references (luxalgo, algostorm, tradeology, tra-mada order-flow/volume-profile guides, united-daytraders absorption/exhaustion study) - cross-checked against each other, no single-source claims kept.

**FOUNDATION SEAL (`2026-11-12`, verified against GitHub the same minute):** MAIN VERSION = commit `0901a6b` (Book `a9697876` @ 3001 lines, ruler `a8925e51` @ 4998). Freeze objects confirmed on the remote: tag `v1.0-pre-strategy-2026-11-12` (annotated `92c5a97` -> `0901a6b`) and read-only branch `freeze-2026-11-12`. All strategy work happens on `strategy-v2` (born from `0901a6b`, also on the remote); the tag/freeze branch are glass cases - NEVER a commit target; walk-back = one line (`git switch v1.0-pre-strategy-2026-11-12`). Owner decision on record: the pre-Oct-31 `main` line is out of scope, left untouched, its 104 old commits archived by git itself; if ever wanted, a reconciliation chapter, no rush. Demo loop continues sailing from the working folder while it stays byte-identical (planning/Book-only); the moment strategy touches robot code, decide run-location first (keep-old-system brief).

### Entry 1 - AUCTION / BALANCE / IMBALANCE (`2026-11-12`)
The market is a two-way auction whose job is to ADVERTISE price until business gets done. Where time+volume pile up, price is ACCEPTED (= value). Where price pokes and nobody trades there, it is REJECTED. BALANCE = both sides agree: price rotates, profile is round/bell (D-shaped), value areas of consecutive sessions OVERLAP, point of control crossed freely, VWAP flat. Roughly 70% of session time per CME-data studies - the memory only keeps the 30%. IMBALANCE = one side aggressive enough to force the auction to SEARCH for new value: elongated profile, one-timeframing (a level touched only once, never traded again that session), value MIGRATING day over day, VWAP sloping with price, range extension backed by strong relative volume and directional delta. Acceptance vs failure test: after leaving the old value area, do time AND volume build outside it (new value forming -> run continues) or does it wick back inside (probe failed -> rotation back to old POC). **Vocabulary collision, law:** the classroom's 3/6/9/12 "imbalance" is a BAR-level footprint diagonal; AMT "imbalance" is a DAY-state. In this book say `bar-imbalance` vs `day-imbalance`, never just "imbalance". For our engine: `Regime(TREND/RANGE/flat_lake/vr_chop)` is already a day-state detector - AMT is the theory behind it, and it sits ABOVE every setup (the profitable behaviors in the two states are near opposites).

### Entry 2 - THE TOOLKIT: VOLUME PROFILE / VAH / VAL / POC / VWAPs / S-R (`2026-11-12`)
**Volume Profile** = the histogram laid sideways: volume traded AT EACH PRICE (not per time - that is what makes it an auction transcript). **POC** = the single price with the most volume: the fairest price, behaves as magnet + support/resistance. **Value Area (VA)** = the contiguous band holding ~70% of the period's volume (70% = one std-dev 68.2% rounded by Steidlmayer), built centered on POC; **VAH/VAL** = its upper/lower edge = primary fade levels in balance and the doorway for initiative in imbalance: above VAH WITH volume = initiative buying, above VAH on thin volume = probe to fade. **VWAP** = volume-weighted average price: the running live consensus (dynamic fair value); in balance price rotates around a flat VWAP, a VWAP sloping away with price is the average's vote that imbalance took over. **"WVAP1/VWAP2" decoded for the owner:** session VWAP (starts at day open) and ANCHORED VWAP (starts at any event you choose: news, swing low, yesterday's POC) - many platforms list them as two tools plus deviation bands; we own `vwap_c` live already. **SUPPORT/RESISTANCE, auction flavor:** not drawn lines but acceptance facts - HVNs (volume hills) defend, LVNs (valleys) get traversed FAST again because the market already said "not fair here"; walls/doors are our version. Already in the boat: memory_doors = POC/VA/HTF_POC/OB feed the TP ladder (`shooting_team.py` step 3), `PM_HTF_POC_ENABLE=1`, and scout `Door Reach & Hold` ([8e]: reached 41-43%, held 37-45%) is literally measuring acceptance vs rejection per door.

### Entry 3 - ORDER FLOW: DELTA / ABSORPTION / EXHAUSTION (`2026-11-12`)
Every trade has an aggressor: market orders CROSS the spread and move price; limit orders WAIT and soak. **DELTA** = aggressive-buy volume minus aggressive-sell volume inside one bar; **CVD** = the running total (session aggression ledger). **Delta divergence** = price prints a new high while delta fades/negative (buyer fatigue) or new low while delta strengthens (seller exhaustion). **ABSORPTION** = heavy aggression + NO price movement - a passive defender is taking everything offered (big volume at one level, price locked in a tight range): aggression meeting a WALL. **EXHAUSTION** = aggression drying up: each push prints thinner at the extreme, delta shrinks wave over wave, candle closes off its high: a TIRED ENGINE. A selloff can stop because someone catches it (absorption) or because nobody is left to throw (exhaustion) - both end moves, only absorption says there is a buyer underneath. One bar-imbalance is noise; stacked bar-imbalances + absorption/exhaustion AT a key level (VWAP/VAH/VAL/P-previous-H-L) is the signal; absorption at the extreme is often the pair-mate (absorbed then giving up = highest-quality reversal). **Boat map:** the melting ladder IS an absorption/exhaustion machine on the defenders' side - `solid/eroding/melting/melting-fast` = aggression eating a wall, with `melting-fast` = wall about to exhaust; power sensors `cvd_momentum` (delta trend), `l3_aggr_limit` (aggression vs resting limit = absorption candidate), `footprint_levels/big_prints` exist but are NOT yet promoted into any gate ([8f] row 4: cvd_momentum 47.6% WR -0.24 - weak AS SENSOR, untested AS CONTEXT).
**Strategy research agenda (measure-first, laws stay armed):** (a) replay [8e] split by day-state (balance vs imbalance): does door HOLD only mean something in balance? (b) absorption signature (high burn-rate with price NOT breaking) as a door-quality feature vs current reach/hold; (c) CVD slope at signal time as gate CONTEXT (divergence guard), never as a new threshold without [8n]-style episodes; (d) anchored-VWAP at yesterday's POC as the memory-door candidate the TP ladder should prefer; (e) all four get bars written BEFORE any live gate moves. ORDER: the three entries above stand as the shared vocabulary for the coming big system change; when it lands, this ledger gets a new numbered entry and the STEP 2f/2g open files are re-graded on fresh tapes.

### Entry 4 - THE THREE-GATE COMMITTEE (rehearsal, `2026-11-12`): story -> job -> code, never the reverse
Owner law for this chapter: each gate gets (1) a five-year-old story, (2) its job + rule table, and ONLY THEN (3) code. Gate order FROZEN by owner: G1 Auction (balance/imbalance) -> G2 levels (POC, VAL, VAH, VWAP1=session, VWAP2=anchored-to-yesterday's-POC, S/R = value edges & prior extremes, never drawn-wishes) -> G3 order flow (delta, absorption, exhaustion), then ONE PROMISE (frozen TP - existing law). Every gate is a VETO; approval requires all three; nothing is deleted from the current engine, the committee stands in FRONT of the shooter. Bank-check accepted with 3 registered risks: day-type is a live hypothesis (never hard-commit before the profile develops), correlated-witness trap (G1 = shape of the DAY, G3 = the fight at the LEVEL, separate jobs), funnel shrinks trade count (NEVER tune thresholds to force trades - measure first).
**GATE 1 story (the marketplace):** at dawn the bazaar opens; the town wants ONE thing - a fair price where business gets done. Traders shout prices up and down = ADVERTISING. When most people trade there, the town SETTLES (a fat bell forms = BALANCE - today's playbook: bounce off the edges). When one side refuses the fair price and drags the crowd to a new square, the bell goes THIN and TALL and value MIGRATES (IMBALANCE - playbook: walk WITH the search). A poke nobody trades at = REJECTION (come home); price that stays out with real business = ACCEPTANCE (new fair). A corner touched ONCE with no trades = one-timeframe - the town says "we will remember that". UNDECIDED (neutral day) = the auction itself shrugs -> the robot does NOT guess, it stands down.
**GATE 1 job:** watch the developing day and keep ONE label updated every cycle: `BALANCE / SEARCH / UNDECIDED` + a strength score (POWER-style, never a 10:15 hard verdict). Inputs, all reusable from data the replay already holds: profile shape (minute-volume histogram), Initial Balance (first-hour range), open position vs YESTERDAY's value area (in-value = rotation bias; outside-and-auctioning = search bias), one-timeframe count (minutes traded exactly once), value migration vs yesterday's POC/VA. Output: the day-type does NOT pick the fish - it picks WHICH RULES MAY PICK THE FISH: rotation playbooks only on BALANCE, initiative playbooks only on SEARCH, no new positions while UNDECIDED.
**GATE 1 rehearsal (no code until it wins):** ruler labels the two pinned seas + forward demo days from RAW data, then re-scores: bar (to freeze in this Book BEFORE the tape): the committee's yes/no + playbook choice must beat the always-on shooter on BOTH seas in net pts, with trade-count shrink band agreed in advance, >=8 episodes/window per law; predictions on record before results. Status: G1 story DONE, G1 job DONE, rules under owner review - CODE NOT STARTED, LIVE NOT TOUCHED.
**GATE 1 modes, final shape (owner `2026-11-12`, "more explanation, and the gate must ALWAYS answer for the next gate"):** three moods only - BELLY (balance, fat bell, two-sided), WALK (imbalance, thin tall tower, one-sided search), SLEEP (undecided, town shrugs). ACCEPTANCE/REJECTION are NOT moods - they are the two WITNESES that promote SLEEP to WALK (business building outside yesterday's bell = acceptance = WALK; a poke nobody trades = rejection = stays BELLY or back to SLEEP). The gate is a BROADCASTER, never a silent judge: it always posts one message per cycle, in writing: `MOOD=BELLY|WALK|SLEEP | CERTAINTY=0-100 | MOVING=SETTLING|FIRM|FLIPPING | SINCE=<cycle>`. Silence does not exist at this desk; SLEEP is itself an answer, and the next gates MUST read it (G2 under BELLY accepts edge-locations only; under WALK accepts along-the-walk locations only; under SLEEP it writes "soft" on its own answer; G3 same rule for trigger style).
**GATE 1 SIGNED ROSTER (every judge re-read from `judge_panel.py` patterns THIS DAY; one witness per desk):** HEAD WITNESS: `power_v2_regime` M5-ADX (TREND>=25/RANGE<20/UNKNOWN) = strength meter. GUARD WITNESSES: `shadow_flat_lake` (ER<0.22 -> hard BELLY), `shadow_vr_chop` (-> SLEEP-leaning). SLOPE WITNESS: `vwap_trend` (its note shows price-vs-VWAP day after day; the SLOPE is mood, the DISTANCE is not - distance (`vwap_zscore`, `vwap_bands`) is hereby MOVED to G2 where it belongs as location). MIGRATION WITNESSES (re-mapped honestly from yesterday's draft, which had them on G2): `poc_day`, `value_area`, `htf_poc` vote for G1 ONLY in their compare-with-yesterday form (is today's bell on yesterday's bell or next door?); the SAME judges' here-and-now level form stays available to G2 as raw data, flagged `migration=0` in its notes so the two desks read different fields of one witness, never the same vote twice. EVIDENCE WITNESSES: `volume_roc` (business building outside = acceptance fuel), `news_sentiment` (the town-crier FLIPPER), `macro_dxy`/`macro_vix`/`macro_risk` at 0.25 weight (day-long weather, not minute-mood). NEW MEMBERS (exactly two, owner-approved only if rehearsal proves the need): `day_clock` (Initial Balance vs day range - merged with `open_vs_yVA` in one ledger line, because yesterday's value area is the ONE input the robot cannot recompute from today's tape). NOT SIGNED: `trend_macd`, `sma20` (correlated with ADX - two voices saying one thing = one voice wearing two hats), POWER's UP/DOWN voice (direction is the shooter's lean, echoed into the mood message as `LEAN=`, not counted as a witness), `whale_walls`/`whale_balanced`/`queue_pos`/`microprice`/`supply_demand` (place-of-business -> G2), `absorption`/`iceberg*`/`spoof*`/`sweep`/`l3_*`/`footprint_*`/`delta_pressure`/`cvd_*` (the fight at the level -> G3). STATUS: G1 roster SIGNED for rehearsal only - CODE NOT STARTED, LIVE NOT TOUCHED.

**GATE 2 story + job (`2026-11-12`, owner: story first, same drill):** the town's IMPORTANT PLACES - the committee must only act AT a place, never in open fields (a signal in the middle of nowhere has no story behind it). PLACES, kid-words: **the fountain (POC)** = where the most business ever happened, a magnet; **the street doors (VAH/VAL)** = edges of the 70% trading street - in BELLY days you bounce off them, in WALK days crossing them with business = acceptance; **today's running price (VWAP1)** = the fair average so far, moves with the day; **the remembered price (VWAP2, anchored)** = the same average but its clock starts at a chosen big moment (owner choice to make at rules-time: yesterday's fountain OR today's first shout); **friendly floor / mean ceiling (support/resistance)** - but ONLY the auction-honest kind: a floor exists where BUYERS actually stood (wall/wall-ledge/valley edge), never a crayon line; **the skipped shelf (FVG, NEW member)** = three candles where the middle one ran so fast candle-1's wick and candle-3's wick never overlap - an untraded gap, marked wick-to-wick, its 50% line (Consequent Encroachment) is where the town usually comes back to touch; invalidation is a BODY closing through, a mere wick tap does NOT fill it. FVG is honestly the SAME animal as the auction tradition's one-timeframe / low-volume node, just measured on candles instead of minutes - it is admitted, but only after rehearsal (no academic proof exists for it; our own replay already counts touched-once minutes, so the FVG row will be scored next to it and must earn its chair).
**THE PARKED NOTE, placed (owner asked: where do acceptance/rejection belong? -> exactly here too):** the gap's life-cycle IS acceptance/rejection at place-scale: price pokes the skipped shelf and business returns = ACCEPTANCE of the level -> location valid; price touches and is thrown back immediately = rejection -> location spent. Same two witnesses, now testifying at Gate 2 (G1 keeps its vote-for-mood version; one witness, one field, never the same vote twice).
**GATE 2 job:** take the shout (`MOOD/CERTAINTY/MOVING/LEAN`) and answer ONE question per cycle, in writing: `LOCATION=<name of best place> | DIST_ATR=<x | QUALITY=strong|ok|weak | SOFT=0/1 (true when G1 says SLEEP or CERTAINTY<floor) | ACCEPTED=0/1 (was this place poked-with-business or poked-and-rejected recently?)`. Under BELLY: only edge-places score (fountain/street doors), open field = reject. Under WALK: along-the-LEAN places score (skipped shelves, far street door in walk direction); against-the-walk edges are DOWNGRADED, not fatal. G3 may override a `weak`, never a `SOFT`+`weak` pair.
**GATE 2 ROSTER (from the signed panel + fresh code read):** `poc_day` (distance to magnet), `value_area` (at the doors?), `htf_poc` (its here-and-now form: H1 within 1 ATR = magnet w.7, H4 within 1.5 ATR = strong magnet w.9 - config.py:771-773), `supply_demand`, `whale_walls` + `whale_balanced` (business AT the place), `queue_pos`/`microprice` (who's winning the book right here - light weights), `vwap_zscore` + `vwap_bands` (distance from today's price - MOVED IN from G1 by roster law), the Scout doors/iceberg-truth (`iceberg*`, `spoof*` - a spoofed wall is a trap, never a place), and NEW: `gap_finder` (wick-to-wick FVG ledger, CE line, body-close invalidation). NOT signed here: migration forms of poc/value (that's G1's desk), `sweep`/`absorption`/`l3_*`/`footprint_*`/`cvd_*` (G3's fight). STATUS: G2 story DONE, job DONE, roster SIGNED for rehearsal - CODE NOT STARTED, LIVE NOT TOUCHED.
**GATE 2 completeness audit (`2026-11-12`, owner asked "what did we forget?" - re-checked vs sources + own code):** FOUR MISSED PLACES + TWO BORROWINGS added. (1) THE UNTROD FOUNTAIN (naked/virgin POC): yesterday's busiest price that nobody came back to = an unfinished errand, the strongest magnet in the profile tradition (used until tested, then DROPPED) - the memory book's `poc_day` entries are exactly this animal, so it's not new code, only new furniture: the place-list must carry `naked_poc` with a `tested->retire` rule. (2) YESTERDAY'S TWIN WALLS (prior-day HIGH and LOW): the auction guides carry them as the standard cross-session references alongside POC/VAH/VAL - add to the place-list (the memory book already logs supply/demand history; a day-extreme line is one more `kind`, trivial). (3) THE BIG-BOYS' DOOR (session opens: Asian box 00-07 UTC, London open): the Asian-range VOTE was deleted v5.8.1 as noisy (`step2_market_analysis.py:4559`) - but the BOX was a place, not a mood; re-admitted ONLY as place-furniture with no vote, rehearsal will decide if it earns rent. (4) THE FRESHNESS STAMP: a place is strong the FIRST time it's touched; third touch = tired floor (levels die by testing) - the melting-ladder already counts attacks on our walls, so add `touch_count` to the gap/place ledger: 1->strong, 2->ok, 3->weak. BORROWINGS (no new code anywhere): the ACCEPTED=0/1 field is ANSWERED TODAY by `tools/insight.py:204 classify_market_trick` (FAKEOUT_WIN=shook then accepted, TRAP_REVERSAL=baited then rejected, CLEAN_FOLLOW=accepted walk, STRONG/CLEAN_AGAINST, FLAT_CHOP) - it runs every shot already and only needs its label written into the G2 card. And when day-POC, H1-POC and H4-POC CLUSTER at one price the guides call it a structural fortress -> `place_strength` bonus when >=2 fountain kinds agree within 0.5xATR (cheap comparison of numbers the boat already holds). Everything else from the literature (order blocks, BISI/SIBI naming, killzones) is either already on the roster under its boat-name (supply_demand) or stays OUT - the ledger law holds: fewer, independent witnesses. **GATE 2 RANKING + WAIT RULE (`2026-11-12`, owner's idea checked vs sources, approved with two clarifications):** owner's design - "no place, no pass: price must STAND on a step, else wait" - is exactly the institutional checklist tradition (a signal in the open field has no story; only trade levels that agree with structure) and the desks' own tier lists: PDH/PDL are the anchor levels every prior-day algorithm monitors [1](https://www.nexusindicator.com/blog-posts/prior-day-levels-trading-guide.html), session VWAP is THE most-referenced intraday line (cost basis institutions defend) [3](https://xbtfx.com/blog/what-is-vwap-in-trading-vwap-meaning/). IMPORTANCE LADDER as our robot should rank (books + our own tape): 1) **live book walls/doors** (the town is trading THERE NOW - and only our boat can see them; scout tape: reached 41-43%, held 37-45%), 2) **PDH/PDL** (yesterday's fences), 3) **prior VAH/VAL**, 4) **naked/virgin POC** (magnet, spent after first real touch -> retire rule), 5) **session VWAP1** (+ its +-1SD bands as stretch markers), 6) **fortress** (>=2 fountain kinds within 0.5xATR = promotion of the member it contains), 7) **VWAP2 anchored** (owner ribbon pending), 8) **FVG skipped shelf** (rehearsal member; must out-score touched-once minutes to keep its chair), 9) **night box edges** (furniture, no vote - our own history says its voter form was noisy). Ranks 1-3 are HARD steps, 4-8 SOFT steps (need a friend: a wall or a door nearby), 9 = map paint only. MODIFIERS, not places: freshness (touch 1/2/3 -> strong/ok/weak), ACCEPTED flag (fed by `classify_market_trick` labels - shakeout-win/bait-trap), SOFT flag from G1's SLEEP/low-certainty.
**TWO CLARIFICATIONS to the owner's rule (signed):** (a) standing ON a place is PERMISSION, not ORDER - G2 says "you MAY look here", the trigger (a hold, an absorption, a follow-through) is GATE 3's job; a trade taken just because price touched a step is the disease of every young trader, the books say it plainly (wait for reaction AT the level, confirm lower [2](https://www.tradingview.com/script/kjEr6l5b-Institutional-7-Point-Checklist/)). (b) "WAIT" is not a frozen fish: the robot re-asks the gate EVERY cycle, so the moment price arrives at a step the gate opens - nothing is lost, only noise is. And "at a step" gets a definition, or it is a feeling: `|price-place| <= 0.75 x ATR` (near = inside the step's yard; the 0.15-ATR buffer law still decides where the LINE goes, not whether we're AT the place). All of this rehearsal-scored via the counterfactual log: episodes where G2 would have said WAIT get recorded and graded next to the ones it let through (>=8/window law, no live change without a both-seas win). CODE NOT STARTED, LIVE NOT TOUCHED.
**G2 REAL-NAME GLOSSARY (owner request `2026-11-12` - story names always paired with the finance names, in the ladder's order):** 1) live walls = **live L3 order-book whale walls / BookMap structural doors** (SCOUT_VIABLE_DOOR, wall shelter, `road_clear`); 2) yesterday's two fences = **PDH / PDL - Prior Day High, Prior Day Low** (the anchor levels); 3) yesterday's street doors = **prior session VAH / VAL (Value Area High/Low, the 70% edges)**; 4) the Untried Fountain = **naked / virgin / untested POC (prior Point of Control)** - the magnet; 5) Today's Running Price = **session VWAP** (+/-1 SD VWAP deviation bands); 6) the Fortress = **POC confluence** (daily + H1/H4 POC cluster); 7) the Remembered Price = **anchored VWAP (AVWAP)** - VWAP re-anchored at a chosen event; 8) the skipped shelf = **Fair Value Gap (FVG, 3-candle imbalance; 50% line = consequent encroachment/CE)** - same animal as the TPO books' **low-volume node (LVN) / one-time-frame** at auction scale; 9) the Night Corner = **Asian range / overnight box** (00:00-07:00 UTC, the London-open reference).
**THE M3 SCALPER FRAME LAW (owner instruction, governs EVERY gate, not just G2):** the robot scalps the 3-minute chart (active setup M3 +3/6/9/12m - the replay's own frame). Design consequences: (a) G1 is the ONLY day-scale member - context reads slow, the committee acts fast (correct split for a scalper, never the reverse); (b) a step only counts if REACHABLE: the 0.75xATR yard is measured in M3 waves (a typical demo wave ~3.5-4 pts -> the yard is ~3 pts - about ONE M3 bar's body, no fantasy walks), and no place farther than 2 M3-ATR from price enters the candidate list; (c) all level math per-cycle must stay cheap (nearest-place lookup, touch counters, distance compare - no re-profiling inside the loop, MTF+speed directive); (d) freshness decays FAST at M3: touch-count uses TODAY's touches only (yesterday's wear is forgotten - a scalper re-feels the floor fresh each session); (e) FVG at M3 scale is where noise lives: its rehearsal bar includes the minimum-size rule (owner to choose in waves) precisely because a 3-min chart makes mouse-holes everywhere. Glossary style now stands as a house rule: every story name keeps its finance twin beside it in this Book from now on.
**G2 DESIGN Q&A (owner `2026-11-12`, two questions, answers signed into the ladder):** (Q1) *prior-day VA/POC or today's developing ones?* BOTH, but in different chairs: **yesterday's settled lines (prior VAH/VAL/naked POC) = HARD map** - a verdict the whole town already paid for, it CANNOT move under the robot's feet -> best used as reaction places and TARGETS; **today's developing value = SOFT shadow** - still being voted every bar, so it may inform but never gate; it gets exactly two duties: (a) rotation CONFIRMER (developing VA ~flat + price inside it = the BELLY vote strengthens - feeds G1's evidence, cheap: rolling volume-at-price bins from the M3 tape), and (b) today's edges as SOFT steps only after the first hour (a young profile is noise). The question 'who takes care of today's value?' has its own answer already: **session VWAP + its SD bands ARE today's fair-value line in institutional form** (the desks benchmark against VWAP, not against the developing TPO), and **the live walls are today's value as the book actually trades it** - so NO separate intraday-POC rung is added; the developing VA stays a soft helper, one rung at most. (Q2) *where are support & resistance?* - S/R is NOT a rung, it is the NAME OF THE WEATHER: every rung IS a support or a resistance (fences=strongest S/R in the books, doors, fountain=both sides, VWAP=defended S/R). A separate 'S/R lines' step was deliberately refused as double-counting (the ladder IS the S/R list, ranked and alive); what the ladder keeps from the S/R family: the live walls (hard), yesterday's fences and doors (hard), `supply_demand` zones from the memory book (SOFT step, weight 0.6 - the honest kind of drawn S/R), and classic floor-pivot R/S numbers + plain round numbers = MAP PAINT only (furniture, no vote). LADDER FINAL: HARD: 1 live walls/doors (L3 whale walls, SCOUT doors) · 2 PDH/PDL · 3 prior VAH/VAL; SOFT: 4 naked POC (target+magnet, retire-on-first-real-touch) · 5 session VWAP +/-1SD · 6 developing-VA edges (post-first-hour only) · 7 supply_demand zones · 8 fortress bonus (not a rung, a gold star on 2+3+4+5) · 9 anchored VWAP (ribbon pending owner) · 10 FVG (rehearsal, size rule pending); PAINT: 11 Asian box, round numbers, floor pivots. Still owner-pending before rules freeze: ribbon anchor (Q1), FVG min size (Q2), sleep-day fortress (Q3). CODE NOT STARTED, LIVE NOT TOUCHED.
**G2 LADDER AMENDMENT - the research surprise (`2026-11-12`, "what else does a similar job?"):** a sweep of the level-literature (official settlement as the mark-to-market reference every clearing house moves on [1](https://senzoukria.com/blog/settlement-price-explained)[3](https://spxtospy.com/articles/es-futures-daily-settlement), session opens as daily-bias anchors and the settlement->open gap as overnight repositioning, overnight highs/lows as liquidity pools) found that OUR BOAT ALREADY BUILDS THE WHOLE FAMILY: `session_levels.py` is named `THE JUDGE` and computes, cheap + restart-safe, exactly: `prev_settlement` (COMEX 13:30 ET window average), `Globex open 18:00 ET`, `midnight open`, `ONH/ONL` (overnight high/low 18:00-08:20 ET), `IBH/IBL` (COMEX initial balance 08:20-09:20 ET) - and `signal_team.py:655` already pays a wall +0.15 resistance when it forms AT one of those anchors. So the amendment is promotions, not new sensors: LADDER +2 SOFT steps: **the Overnight Box (ONH/ONL)** - the institutional, already-computed form of what this page earlier called the 'Asian night corner' (that 00-07 UTC box in step2 stays PAINT; ONH/ONL is the real surveyed box) - and **the Day's First Shout (today's open: Globex 18:00 / midnight)** - above it = the day bought its tickets expensive, below = cheap; a reaction place AND the ribbon-candidate for Q1 (owner to choose). MODIFIER +1: the **Empty Hallway rule** (LVN, from the AMT books): a valley of no-business between price and a far step means price TRAVELS FAST through it - the ladder must not place a TARGET one step across a hallway without an extra confirm (hallways are run-throughs, not standings); computable from the minute-volume bins G1 already builds. G1 evidence +1: the **settlement->open gap** as overnight-repositioning strength (feeds the mood's acceptance vote, per the FibAlgo institutional cycle note [2](https://www.tradingview.com/script/FtUiGFtE-FibAlgo-ICT-Midnight-Open-True-Day/)). REFUSED (same job in cheaper clothes): floor pivots (paint), weekly/monthly POC (already inside the fortress rule), volume-profile-from-yesterday duplicate (prior VAH/VAL/POC cover it), naked VWAP (a ribbon, already Q1). Three owner questions unchanged and re-told to the owner in story form; answers pending. CODE NOT STARTED, LIVE NOT TOUCHED.
**G2 ANSWERS LOGGED (`2026-11-12`, owner answers + research, three questions closed or parked):**
(Q1 - VWAP, owner answered with his own experience) owner asked about the +1/-1 lines: named = **VWAP standard-deviation bands** (his old play = *VWAP band fade / mean-reversion to the center*). RULE SIGNED: the robot plots bands 1/2/3 (cheap sigma math, the session VWAP line itself needs no new sensor) but **uses 1 and 2 as places** (owner's two-each-side preference adopted); band 3 is NOT a place - it is a WARNING: a push beyond band-3 with business = today is a day-state RE-VOTE (the books agree: beyond band 3 is 'no longer a stretch, a trend day starting - fading it gives back a week in an afternoon' [3](https://affordableindicators.com/articles/vwap-trading-strategy-futures/)). HARD PAIRING LAW from the research: **the band fade is a BELLY-day playbook only** - flat VWAP + price tags band 2 + a closing candle back INSIDE the band = the trigger (never touch it while VWAP SLOPES; on trend days a band-2 tag is CONTINUATION, not a fade [1](https://www.financialtechwiz.com/post/vwap-tradingview/)) - a perfect demonstration of why Gate 1 stands before Gate 2: the SAME line is a trampoline on one mood and a catapult on the other. Sub-question (the ribbon's START line, clarified for the owner as 'which morning-call does today begin at'): provisional = **18:00 ET Globex open** (our `session_levels.py` already rolls sessions exactly there; `midnight open` stored as switchable alternative) - marked PROVISIONAL-DEFAULT, not owner-chosen.
(Q2 - FVG size) owner decision: LEARN-BY-PRACTICE, no desk guess. SIGNED: for the M3 scalp frame the shelf must be **>= 1 x ATR(M3)** to be drawn at all (a wave is the boat's own breath - it fits the timeframe by construction); size is logged as `fvg_atr` (gap-in-waves) on every rehearsal episode and **re-examined after 30 demo days** - the ladder annotation `size-rule: PENDING-LONG-STUDY (1xATR provisional)` is permanent until the data votes. Noted honestly: the band stats (68/95/99.7% inside 1/2/3 sigma) are a NORMALITY CONVENTION, not a law of markets [2](https://finwiz.io/technical-indicators/vwap) - treated as ruler-thresholds, never as physics.
(Q3 - sleep day + fortress) owner asked for research, then a pick matching our design. RESEARCH: the day-types tradition treats the undecided (neutral) day as 'stand down or size down' [1](https://footprint-lab.com/guides/market-profile/) - the auction itself is undecided; a sub-school fades extensions toward POC on neutral days ONLY while responsive flow keeps winning, and honors the 'neutral EXTREME' (close at an edge + value shift) as tomorrow's fuel [2](https://algostorm.com/market-profile/). DECISION SIGNED (option C, the middle path): on SLEEP the committee is closed **EXCEPT** when the fortress holds, RIGHT NOW, a live wall wearing the ACCEPTED stamp - then, and only then, one fish at HALF size; a single touch with no stamp remains a NO. The neutral-extreme lesson is parked for the next chapter (an edge-close on a sleep day = tomorrow's opening evidence - belongs to G1's day-book, not tonight's ladder). **GATE 3 RESEARCH - THE FIGHT AT THE STEP (owner `2026-11-12` session; reliable sources studied; house rule kept: every story name carries its finance twin).** Owner's three-gate meaning CONFIRMED, with the link between gates signed: G1 = the town's mood; G2 = price must stand within 0.75xATR of a rung or NO PASS (re-asked every M3 cycle) - and when it passes, G2 hands the rung's NAME forward; G3 = the only place where bounce-vs-break is decided FOR THAT NAMED RUNG. **WHAT G1's MOOD IS FOR (owner asked):** the mood never opens the door - it decides WHAT A DOOR MEANS and HOW BIG THE BET. BELLY (balance): a rung touch is FADE material, first touch usually holds, target back to the fountain (POC/VWAP). WALK (trend): a rung tag is a CONTINUATION mark, do NOT fade it (band law: never fight a sloping VWAP), target = the NEXT rung over. SLEEP (neutral): a rung alone is not enough - middle-path half-size or stand aside (Q3 answer). Same street, same step, three different trades - this is why the mood card is read first.
**G3 ROSTER - THE THREE OWNED MEMBERS (every sensor EXISTS and votes; weights read live from step2_market_analysis.py `_JUDGE_DEFAULT_W`):** DELTA (the shouting match, aggression meter): `footprint_delta` w1.0, `l3_net_flow` w1.5, `delta_pressure` w0.6, `cvd_momentum` w0.5; law: at the rung the shouting must point WITH the hoped move OR contradict price - `cvd_divergence` w0.6 is the dedicated lie-detector (price pokes a new high, CVD refuses to follow = legs gone). ABSORPTION (the wall eating bites): `absorption` w0.6 fed by `OrderFlowMetrics.absorption_events/absorption_net` (step2:1587-1907: a resting wall losing size while price refuses to budge = someone eats everything), `iceberg` w1.0 (+`iceberg_noise` demux) as hidden-appetite engine, `sweep` w0.9 for the just-cleared-pool meaning change. EXHAUSTION (tired legs): `volume_roc` w0.4 (each push on less air) + the OFI streak decay in `l3_ofi_streak` w1.0.
**EXTRA TOOLS THE RESEARCH FOUND - SAME JOB (bounce-or-break at a level) - ADOPTED INTO G3, pinned one by one:** (1) ORDER FLOW IMBALANCE (OFI, queue imbalance) - the academic crown: Cont-Kukanov-Stoikov, J. Financial Econometrics 2014: short-horizon price change is nearly LINEAR in order-flow imbalance, slope inversely proportional to book depth; multi-level OFI adds real power out-of-sample. VERIFIED IN OUR CODE, ALREADY VOTING: cumulative OFI fields (step2:985,1018), tanh(ofi/booksize) vote (:3757), distance-decayed `near_touch_ofi` (:2162-2168), judges `l3_ofi_streak`/`l3_large_ofi` - the boat has traded with the paper for months without naming it. LAW: OFI's predictability is SECOND-SCALE and contemporaneous (execution info, not conviction info) => G3 uses it ONLY as the GO/NO-GO tie at the step moment, never as a hold-the-fish signal. (2) UNFINISHED BUSINESS (unfinished auction): if the extreme of a push prints ~zero traded volume at its last tick, the auction did not finish there - revisit likely; footprint-native, needs only a tiny tail-zero check on our existing footprint bins. (3) TRAPPED TRADERS / THE SNAP-BACK: a break that reclaims the level within 1-2 M3 closes turns the breakers into forced fuel - the classic fade-with-them trade; OUR WITNESS EXISTS: `classify_market_trick` already stamps FAKEOUT_WIN / TRAP_REVERSAL every shot - it becomes G3's bounce-side evidence beside the ACCEPTED stamp. (4) EFFORT vs RESULT (Wyckoff) and STOPPING VOLUME: huge volume + tiny travel = hands changing = absorption's loud cousin; free from our tape as `effort_result` = per-minute volume / |ticks moved|, spike at a rung = stopping volume (tiny calc, no new sensor). (5) WALL ADD-or-PULL before arrival (heatmap logic): a wall GROWING as price approaches = real guard (bounce); a wall PULLED at the last moment = empty doorway (break) - our `burn_word` ladder (eroding/melting/melting-fast) on whale walls already reads the pull side; G3 reads its SPEED as a break-odds dial, and a live iceberg flips a pulled-looking wall back to BOUNCE (appetite hiding, not leaving). (6) THE TOLL / ACCEPTANCE LAW (excess, Market Profile): the rung is NOT broken until price pays the toll - NexusFi futures law: 2 consecutive 1-3min closes beyond, OR 5+ minutes holding without reclaim, with volume building beyond; a poke-and-snap-back = liquidity SWEEP, not a break (profile words: look-above-and-fail); anti-chase clause: PASS if price is already > half the 5-period ATR beyond the rung, or the breakout bar already ate >75% of the expected move (luxalgo: confirmation shifts odds, never removes failure; each extra close filters more fails but worsens entry - the trade-off is chosen, not discovered). Pure DECISION LOGIC: free, no sensor. (7) OPEN INTEREST (the new-money meter): classic futures law, all three sources agree - break + RISING OI = fresh commitment (credible); break + FALLING OI = short-covering / long-unwinding = borrowed fuel, fade-ready; OI is CONFIRMATION, never a primary signal. GAP CONFIRMED HONEST: grep across the whole boat = ZERO OI sensors and the L2/MBO feed carries no OI field -> `OI=BLIND` is the Book's official state; PENDING-LONG-STUDY: check broker feed for even DAILY OI snapshots (a day-stamp still kills many fake breaks); the other six tools still decide meanwhile.
**REFUSED for design-fit (same discipline as G2 refusals):** tick-level diagonal stacked-imbalance reading (our M3 bins cannot honestly see it), dark-pool prints (futures have none), options gamma exposure (GC options too thin to steer an M3 scalp), raw tape-print eyeballing (subsumed by volume_roc + delta judges), DOM entry micromanagement (execution belongs to the shooter, not the committee).
**G3 DRAFT JOB CARD (code NOT started, for the rehearsal):** `RUNG=<name handed by G2> | DELTA=+-x | ABSORB=net | LEGS=exhaust0-100 | OFI=+-sign | EFFORT=ratio | WALL=adding|holding|melting | EXCESS=holds:N | TRAP=0/1 | OI=BLIND` -> `VERDICT=BOUNCE | BREAK | UNDECIDED`. **UNDECIDED IS A FIRST-CLASS ANSWER:** the auction is allowed to be silent; the robot stands at the step and re-asks next cycle - no skipping forward, no trade invented from boredom. BOUNCE verdict feeds the shooter's fade rules; BREAK verdict must carry the toll (EXCESS acceptance) before any chase. CODE NOT STARTED, LIVE NOT TOUCHED.
**GATE 3 = THE TRIGGER GATE - senior design review (owner ruling `2026-11-12`: "gate 3 is the most important part of the app - here we find out the move by checking data level 3... here we can send the order of buy or sell").** PROMOTION ACCEPTED and formalized: **G3 is the ONLY voice in the system allowed to say BUY or SELL**; G1/G2 can only ever say PASS/WAIT - they prepare the moment, they never create an order. The boat's own weights table agrees with the owner's instinct: the panel's highest weights are ALL order-flow family (`l3_net_flow` 1.5 > `whale_walls` 1.4 > `footprint_delta` / `l3_ofi_streak` / `iceberg` 1.0 each, `_JUDGE_DEFAULT_W`) - the engine was built trusting the L3 family the most. ONE frozen law kept: **the mouth stays separate from the hand** - G3 emits a signed VERDICT ticket (`VERDICT | RUNG | full G3 card`); the EXISTING shooter turns the ticket into the order with frozen geometry (side=nearest_door, buffer max(2*spread, 0.15*ATR), TP=door-buffer, SL=shelter, rope laws untouched). No new execution path; minimum diff. Reason: rehearsal grades the CARDS, not the fills - if the committee owned geometry too, a loss could never be blamed on either of them.
**CODE-VERIFIED TODAY - the scary parts already have legs:** (a) our Level-3 is real, not a nickname: `Level3Events` (step2:1006-1033) carries `order_events` with true order_id/side/size ("mbo-lifecycle-2026-10-27", :2256), per-price `spoof_events` (large add then cancel <2s), `iceberg_levels` refill counts, buy/sell streaks, `aggressive_flow_ratio`, cumulative `ofi`, distance-decayed `near_touch_ofi` (L3_NEAR_TOUCH_USD). (b) FAIL-CLOSED discipline for L3 is PRE-BUILT: the stage3r gate (:440-460) reads `data_quality.level3_events` and BLOCKS its stage when the MBO feed is `unavailable|stale_or_unverifiable`, plus honesty labels (`partial_mbo_market_order_events`, :5922). G3 adopts this exact pattern as its floor law: the feed must be PROVEN ALIVE before the trigger may speak.
**THE SEVEN FIRE-LAWS (senior checklist for any gate that gets a trigger finger; each maps to an existing mechanism or one new line):** (1) FRESH EYES: fire-time checks snapshot age; stale data is worse than no data (the bot-desk consensus) -> stale book = UNDECIDED, never fire on old eyes. (2) ONE JUMP PER QUESTION (idempotency): client ticket id = (symbol, M3 cycle id, gate) as canonical key, broker order_id stored SEPARATELY, retries dedupe by the ticket - a network timeout after the exchange already took the order is HOW BOTS DOUBLE-FIRE; every order walks the full lifecycle NEW->SUBMITTED->ACKED->PARTIAL->FILLED/CANCELLED; the BROKER is the source of truth, reconcile local state vs broker on startup AND periodically (local books WILL drift). (3) FAIL-CLOSED COMMITTEE: any dead G3 sensor => verdict UNDECIDED => no order; G3's silence is SAFE by design - the deliberate asymmetry with G1 where silence is FORBIDDEN (mood must always speak; the trigger speaks only when sure). (4) LOOK BOTH WAYS (pre-send sanity, enforced in OUR layer - never trust the broker to block harmful orders): limit orders only, price within X% of mid, size caps, velocity caps (max N orders/hour), per-symbol exposure cap, daily-loss lockout, blackout windows around red-folder news (NewsAndEvents is already the alarm clock). (5) KILL SWITCH above everything: manual flatten-and-halt + auto-throttled pause on anomalies (error bursts, p99 latency spikes, fills diverging from expected) - layered defense where EVERY layer assumes the layer above can fail. (6) THE DIARY: every G3 card persisted as JSONL beside the tape, ticket id stamped on the order journal line - Law #8 replay re-grades EVERY jump, miss and false fire (grading classes = fired / missed / false-fired, not just win/lose). (7) DECIDE AT THE CLOSE: verdict computed on M3 bar-close with a fire-time freshness re-check; NO 200 ms scan loop (sealed) - the trigger gate inherits the cycle's clock.
**HONEST GAP FOR THE LEDGER (L3 naming law):** industry L3 = market-by-order (every add/modify/execute/cancel - the feed that makes spoofing and icebergs visible at all; the surveillance-grade data source), and it is ENORMOUS (billions of messages/day is why most desks can't run it raw). Ours is MBO-DERIVED via the BookMap bridge path (`DATA_SOURCE in {bookmapbridge, ninjabridge...}`), quality-DECLARED, and the code itself labels parts `partial_mbo_market_order_events`. THEREFORE: every G3 card carries `L3Q=ok|partial|stale` and the fire-law is absolute: **L3Q != ok -> VERDICT may still be BOUNCE/BREAK (the mouth may speak) but the ticket is NOT FIRED (the hand stays in the pocket)** - quality gate BEFORE the trigger, never after.
**MANAGER'S BOTTOM LINE (one breath):** the promotion is RIGHT and the design is safe to build: (i) every G3 sensor exists and already votes (the roster above), (ii) the two deadliest failure modes - stale feed and double-fire - have pre-built patterns in the boat (stage3r + data_quality) to copy, (iii) mouth/hand split leaves the frozen engine untouched. THE PRICE, signed in advance: MOST M3 cycles will end UNDECIDED with no order - the funnel shrinks trade count; per the frozen funnel law we MEASURE the shrink, we never tune thresholds to force trades. The rehearsal (both pinned seas, beat the always-on shooter, >=8 episodes per window) is this gate's examiner above all gates - the gate that fires must earn its trigger finger on tape, not in the book. CODE NOT STARTED, LIVE NOT TOUCHED.
**GATE 3 MEMBER STORIES - deep dive, code-verified (owner `2026-11-12`: "tell me more about the members, how they work, why important, how they help"). House law kept: story name first, finance twin beside it. Every mechanism below was READ from the live code the same day, with line numbers.**
**M1. THE SHOUTING MATCH - delta** (judges `footprint_delta` w1.0, `l3_net_flow` w1.5, `delta_pressure` w0.6, `cvd_momentum` w0.5). Mechanism: every executed trade has an AGgressor - the impatient one who pays the spread; delta = aggressive-buy-volume minus aggressive-sell-volume, per bar and per price; the streak counters (buy_streak/sell_streak, Level3Events :1019-1020) count WHO IS MORE IMPATIENT IN A ROW. Helps: tells which side is actually in a hurry AT the rung - a poke past a step with nobody paying up is a lie.
**M2. THE BIG KID WHO EATS PUSHES - absorption** (judge `absorption` w0.6 <- `OrderFlowMetrics._update_absorption` step2:1756-1800, THE FINEST SENSOR ON THE BOAT, its own docstring cites real research - Frey & Sandas 2009, Cont & Kukanov 2017). VERIFIED EXACT RULES: wall must be within $0.40 of the touch (so deep-book cancels $5 away cannot fake absorption - :1781-1788), the resting wall must SHRINK while price HOLDS at/against it (best_bid >= wall-0.05 :1790+), bid-absorbing-sellers = BULLISH +1, ask-absorbing-buyers = BEARISH -1, and every event EXPIRES in a rolling 180-second window (morning fights never vote in the afternoon - :1767). Helps: THE strongest bounce witness: the pushers came, the eater ate them all, the step held.
**M3. THE HIDDEN EATER - iceberg** (judges `iceberg` 1.0 + `iceberg_noise` 0.0 demux; data: `iceberg_levels` refill-counts and `iceberg_meta` {refills, first_seen, age_sec, score} in Level3Events :1024-1028). Mechanism: a whale never shows one big ticket - he keeps RE-posting small ones at the same price; the refill count is his footprint. Helps: flips a wall that LOOKS like it's walking away (M6) into an eater that is merely HIDING - turns break-suspicion back into bounce-trust.
**M4. THE TIRED LEGS - exhaustion** (judges `cvd_divergence` 0.6, `volume_roc` 0.4, `l3_ofi_streak` decay). Mechanism: a NEW price extreme WITHOUT a new CVD extreme = divergence (the shout no longer matches the step); volume shrinking push after push = each stair climbed with less air. Helps: the anti-chase alarm - a tired push arriving AT a hard rung is exactly the fade window the Gate-1 mood only promised.
**M5. THE DOOR CREAK - order flow imbalance (OFI)** (`near_touch_ofi` distance-weighted :1029/2163; `l3_ofi_streak` 1.0; tanh(ofi/book-size) vote :3757; science: Cont-Kukanov-Stoikov 2014 - price change ~ LINEAR in book imbalance, slope inverse to depth). Helps: hears the door creak BEFORE it opens - the earliest member; LAW: seconds-scale life => tie-break at the step moment, NEVER a hold-the-fish signal.
**M6. THE SCARECROW WATCHERS - spoof + wall add-or-pull** (judges `spoof_invert` 0.6 + `spoof_invert_loose` 0.4; data: `spoof_events` = big order ADDED then CANCELLED within 2 seconds, Level3Events :1023). Mechanism: a scary wall that vanishes before anyone leans on it is a SCARECROW - business nobody meant; a wall GROWING as price arrives = a real guard; the burn_word ladder (eroding/melting/melting-fast) reads the walking-away speed. Helps: a rung is only REAL if the people standing at it are real - spoofed step = no step; melting = break odds UP; adding = bounce guard confirmed (unless M3 says the eater is merely hiding).
**M7. THE SNAP-BACK TRAP - trapped traders** (witness: `classify_market_trick` FAKEOUT_WIN / TRAP_REVERSAL stamps every shot; friend `sweep` w0.9 for the just-cleared stop pool). Mechanism: a poke beyond the rung grabs the stop-losses sleeping behind it, then price snaps back inside - now the poke-buyers are TRAPPED and must sell at any price to escape; the bounce EATS the trapped. Helps: turns the most painful event (failed break) into the BEST fade setup, and stamps the rung battle-proven (the ACCEPTED stamp).
**M8. THE TOLL - acceptance / excess** (pure decision logic, no sensor; law from NexusFi + luxalgo: 2 consecutive M3 closes beyond OR 5+ minutes holding with volume building beyond = moved in; poke-and-snap = a sweep, the OPPOSITE signal; anti-chase: already > half-ATR beyond = PASS, break bar ate >75% of expected move = the risk is already spent). Helps: stops the classic M3 death - chasing a PEEK. Looking over the fence is not living in the house; only moved-in counts as BREAK.
**M9. UNFINISHED BUSINESS** (tiny tail-zero check on existing footprint bins). Mechanism: a push whose LAST tick traded almost NOTHING left the auction unfinished - the town never got to sell at that price; price habitually COMES BACK to finish the job. Helps: explains WHERE a bounce goes and sets the revisit as a free target.
**M10. THE NEW-MONEY METER - open interest: OFFICIAL STATE = BLIND** (grep: zero sensors; feed carries none; card shows `OI=BLIND`; PENDING-LONG-STUDY: even DAILY snapshots would help). Law if it ever arrives: break + OI RISING = new bettors committed (credible); break + OI FALLING = old losers leaving (borrowed fuel, fade-ready). Helps: the great fake-break filter we currently do WITHOUT.
**M11. THE TWO QUIET HELPERS - microprice + queue_pos** (`microprice` 0.6 = Stoikov size-weighted mid (step2:1642) - the TRUE price when one line of people is much longer than the other; `queue_pos` 0.5 = where our own ticket sits in its line). They serve the HAND, not the mouth: honest entry price, fill probability. They never vote bounce-vs-break; once G3 speaks, they make the order smart.
**THE INDEPENDENCE LAW - why 11 members are a committee, not a choir:** they answer the ONE question with THREE DIFFERENT SENSES: (a) WHO IS IN A HURRY - executed trades (M1, M4's divergence); (b) WHO IS STANDING READY - the resting book (M2, M3, M5, M6); (c) DID THE MARKET MOVE IN - price across time (M7, M8, M9). OI (M10) would be a fourth sense: who OPENED a bet. Delta and CVD are SIBLINGS (same data) - they count as ONE voice, never two witnesses (the correlated-witness trap, G1 law, applies here with full force). VERDICT = when trade-family AND book-family agree AND the toll says so; anything less = UNDECIDED = wait at the step, re-ask next cycle. BOUNCE recipe: pushers refused at the rung + eater eating (M2/M3) + toll UNPAID. BREAK recipe: pushers insisting with big delta + wall melting and no hidden eater + TOLL PAID. Effort:result ratio and unfinished business MODULATE; OI noted absent. CODE NOT STARTED, LIVE NOT TOUCHED.
**GATE 3 AUDIT - THE TWO-DOOR LAW APPLIED (owner `2026-11-12` gave the cleanest definition yet and ordered a one-by-one re-check): G2 = the agents DRAW the lines/areas - the places where a challenge will come; G3 = the agents put THE MICROSCOPE on the live level-3 data AT the drawn line, where price stands NOW, and answer BOUNCE-or-BREAK NOW. Every member M1-M11 re-checked against this law. Verdicts: 5 KEEP, 2 demoted to LAWS, 1 moved to G2, 1 moved to the hand, 1 conflict fixed, 1 unblind plan signed.**
KEEP AT GATE 3 (they only make sense at THIS minute, at THIS line): M1 delta - the pushes are being EXECUTED at the rung right now; M2 absorption - wall eaten while price holds, and its 180-second expiry IS the microscope freshness law in action; M3 iceberg - refills arriving this minute; M5 OFI - the door creak is sub-minute by its nature (Cont-Kukanov-Stoikov); M6 spoof + wall add-or-pull - a scarecrow matters only at the moment price leans on the wall; plus microprice as book-family eye - the live tilt of the true mid is short-scale direction evidence. Pure microscope material, nothing to move.

MOVED TO GATE 2 (the owner caught a real one - signed as HIS correction): M9 unfinished business is a property BORN THE MOMENT THE LEVEL IS BORN (a zero-trade extreme leaves a DEBT on that price; it is drawn WITH the line, decays only on a later visit, and says NOTHING about who wins at the step this minute). It becomes a rung ANNOTATION: the G2 card gains `UOB=0/1` inside its QUALITY field - a PDH/PDL or IB extreme with an unfinished tail gets a MAGNET STAR (quality bump; still not permission to trade). G3 inherits the rung's NAME + QUALITY from the G2 card and NEVER re-measures it. The toy was in the wrong room; now it lives with the map.

RESOLVED: G1 keeps the SESSION story of the fuel; G3's exhaustion uses ONLY rung-local evidence - CVD-divergence at the push into the level + the shrinking size of the last 2-3 pushes (`cvd_divergence`, streak lengths) - the same field is never checked twice by two gates.

UOB star). Eleven became seven-and-two-and-two-beside - the owner's door law made the committee SHARPER, not smaller.**
**OPEN INTEREST - THE UNBLIND PLAN (owner offered to fix it, so the plan is signed, with honest data physics first): intraday per-push OI does not exist for retail ANYWHERE - exchanges restrict it, and feeds claiming it are estimates. What EXISTS and is free: the DAILY stamp - CME end-of-day OI report, Databento `oi_summary` (daily), or the contract-info OI field most futures brokers expose. FIX = tiny `tools/oi_loader.py`: once per day (friend of the midnight SOP) it fetches/parses prior-day OI + 5-day trend for the traded contract and stores `oi_day=up|down|flat` beside session_levels.json; the G3 card's `OI=` field then carries the daily fuel verdict INTO the rung moment - and new-money-vs-running-home IS an overnight property, so daily cadence MATCHES the question. HARD LIMIT SIGNED: on the MT5-CFD path (XAUUSD - the data_label branch at step2:7746) there is NO true open interest BY DEFINITION (a CFD is the bank's private book; no exchange contract ledger exists) - that path keeps `OI=BLIND` forever, and the loader runs on the futures path only. Owner to confirm which feed his live box serves (futures / CFD / both) at rehearsal time; `oi_loader` joins the code-time list beside `gap_finder` and `avwap_acc` as the third tiny new thing in the entire three-gate build. **THE GATE-3-ONLY VOTING LAW - the owner's committee restructure (`2026-11-12`, dictated verbatim: "in our new system we will count the vote only in Gate 3: judges are able to help in another gate too. For gate 1 and gate 2, we check the general things and need information, so any judges can be there and help with providing info. Only gate 3 is in the higher level, and the judges who work there should be specific and selected, as they do some advanced-level jobs and calculation."). SIGNED AS LAW over the three signing pages:**
(1) **INFO IS FREE.** Every judge (and every non-panel witness: power_v2_regime, the shadow guards, session_levels, classify_market_trick...) may inform ANY desk at ANY time. The rung/desk postings in the G1/G2/G3 signing pages stand as PRIMARY-RESPONSIBILITY labels (who is the best first provider of what) and nothing more.
(2) **G1/G2 DECIDE BY MEASUREMENT, NOT BALLOTS.** G1's output = the broadcast MOOD card (CERTAINTY 0-100 is a MEASURED agreement score, not a counted vote); G2's pass = geometric (price within 0.75xATR of a rung's yard) + stamped facts (ACCEPTED, UOB star, quality, freshness counts). Nothing is voted at those desks; everything is computed - which is exactly how the rosters already behaved; the law now says it out loud.
(3) **GATE 3 IS THE ONLY BALLOT BOX - and that is why its club is CLOSED and SELECTED.** The 18 signed eyes (M1 footprint_delta/l3_net_flow/delta_pressure/l3_large_ofi/cvd_momentum; M2 absorption/l3_aggr_limit; M3 iceberg/iceberg_legacy/iceberg_noise; M4 cvd_divergence; M5 l3_ofi_streak/l3_imbalance; M6 spoof_invert/spoof_invert_loose; M7 microprice; toll's footprint_levels; snap-back's sweep) + the two verdict laws (toll gates BREAK; snap-back confirms BOUNCE - LAWS applied ON TOP of the tally, never counted IN it) + the desk-collapse rule (members of one desk merge into ONE ballot voice, so sibling judges like delta and CVD can never double-vote: the independence law becomes MORE important than ever, being the only anti-double-count fence left). An informing judge NEVER becomes a voting judge by standing nearby - adding or removing a G3 voter is an OWNER-SIGNATURE event.
(4) **THE SHOOTER IS UNTOUCHED.** The engine's own 37-judge weighted panel-score runs exactly as frozen (the committee deletes NOTHING; the BENCH trio mtf/trend_macd/sma20 keeps voting THERE - that is the shooter's gut, not a gate).
(5) **FIRE-LAWS UNCHANGED.** A G3 ballot produces an order only through the seven fire-laws + `L3Q` (fresh eyes, one jump per question, fail-closed, look-both-ways, kill switch, diary, decide-at-the-close). UNDECIDED remains the majority answer by design.
WHAT THE LAW QUIETLY SETTLES: the migration-trio vote-post question (both desks just READ the same notes now - the va_prior/va_dev data-split STAYS mandatory, because that is correctness of WHAT a rung is, not fairness of votes), `poc_day.naked` and `migration=0` tagging likewise remain as data hygiene; the `macro_yield` orphan (informs the weather desk, needs no post); and any recorder-law-vs-one-duck wording drift across the three census pages (one sentence now governs them all: **info free, ballot at G3**). A formatting repair rode along: a stray provisional-tags fragment that had been riding the ends of pages since earlier builds now has its own honest line below.
MANAGER'S NOTE (why this is the RIGHT move, not just a simpler one): redundancy is cheap and GOOD where the output is information - more eyes on the mood and the map can only sharpen the picture; redundancy is DANGEROUS where the output is ACTION - correlated ballots are one rumor wearing three hats. Putting the SINGLE ballot box at the trigger desk concentrates every anti-double-count defense exactly where votes become money, and frees G1/G2 from bookkeeping forever. M3 frame law unchanged: G3's advanced math stays the only heavy per-cycle cost, bounded by design; G1/G2 info-feeding reuses numbers the engine already computed, so it costs nothing extra. **A) TOWN LAWS vs RESEARCH - all six stand, verdicts signed:** (1) YARD LAW (stand within 0.75xATR(M3), else WAIT, re-ask each cycle): the concept is textbook - levels are ZONES not lines and "the size of the zone should MATCH YOUR TRADING TIMEFRAME and account for market volatility" (damnpropfirms S/R FAQ for futures, which quotes typical DAILY GC zones of $8-$12 - our yard is that rule scaled to M3 via the engine's own Law #11: 0.75xATR(M3) ~ 2.4-3 pts at observed sea ATRs 3.2-3.9); the exact 0.75 is OURS, flagged for rehearsal measurement (sweep 0.5-1.0 pre-registered; thresholds are measured, never tuned to force trades - frozen funnel law). (2) ONE-DUCK FUSE: anti-double-count from the confluence/independence books (systemly: witnesses must be INDEPENDENT) - the fuse radius 0.05xATR is ours (same-price-at-tick-resolution), harmless either way since stamps merge by max/OR. (3) ACCEPTED STAMP (no credit for a level nobody has bounced off; enter on REJECTION, not on touch): verified twice over - journalplus: "entering on touch means entering into uncertainty; waiting one candle for confirmation is the single highest-impact adjustment" - and that is precisely our G2-permission/G3-trigger split; supplier live: classify_market_trick, tools/insight.py:204. (4) WORN FLOOR (>=3 touches today -> weak): DIRECTLY backed by the literature now named **level degradation**: "each test absorbs the resting limit orders at that price... after the third or fourth test the level is WEAKER, not stronger; first and second tests offer the best bounce probability; adjust conviction DOWN as test count rises" (journalplus S/R guide; same order-consumption mechanism in damnpropfirms FAQ and Rayner's S/R course). Our boat's refinement is mechanism-consistent and BETTER than the books: degradation counts only UNREPLENISHED consumption, and the boat can SEE replenishment - iceberg refills (Level3Events :1024-1028) and wall growth vs melting (burn_word ladder, signal_team.py:216-221). (5) EMPTY HALLWAY (no target across a no-business valley without an extra confirm): textbook LVN doctrine - "LVNs are rejection/acceleration zones; avoid entries in an LVN, no support there; a break into an LVN accelerates price to the NEXT HVN" (NexusFi volume-profile-nodes academy; quantcrawler POC guide agrees), and the books' own decay note ("each visit fills the trough in, eroding its meaning") independently justifies our today-only freshness clock. (6) GOLD STAR (>=2 anchors within 0.5xATR -> quality promotion): confluence verified ("when a horizontal level aligns with... a high-volume node, the resulting confluence strengthens the signal", damnpropfirms) - and the ENGINE ITSELF has voted this law with money long before the committee: +0.15 wall bonus when a wall sits ON poc/vwap/settlement/onh/ibh (signal_team.py:655). # COMPANY 3 — THE NEW FACTORY (the 3-gate engine's home) — *section opened `2026-11-13` by the owner's INVERSION order, verbatim: "imagine we build our second factory next to our old factory; the 3-Gates engine stands in the middle of the hall with nothing else; we check the old factory and see WHICH PART we copy and bring over so our new factory can work end to end." This picture REPLACED the edit-the-old-boat picture and is now THE plan; it made everything simpler and safer.*

🧸 **For a 5-year-old:** the old factory stays exactly as it is - not one wall moves. Next door we build a new factory. In its middle stands our smart owl engine, which can think but cannot see, cannot hear the clock, has no notebook and no diary. So we go shopping in the old factory and every part we need says: "take my TWIN, not me" - the twin is free, you just ask nicely (import). The new factory gets eyes, a brain on loan, a fresh notebook of its own, a clock and a diary - and NO order button at all. At night we open the two diaries side by side and see who was smarter.

## 3.1 The mindmap of the new factory (what stands in the hall)

## 3.2 The factory's own laws (all INHERITED from Company 1's constitution - the new factory obeys the nine, plus three of its own)
N1. **The old factory is never edited.** This law REVISES the landing shape of STRATEGY ENTRY 5: its "one step2 block + one main.py key" is SUPERSEDED - the call site now stands in OUR hall, and old-file edits drop to ZERO. ENTRY 6 (the swap survey) is NOT cancelled - it is the filed future map, used only if and after the Sea votes.
N2. **Diary-only until the Sea says otherwise** (constitution law 7 at full strength: the new factory cannot touch the old one's decisions from the outside - there is no wire between them at all).
N3. **Stop = kill the process.** Nothing to unplug, nothing to uninstall; every organ is one file; the factory is small and reversible (law 9) by construction.
